> **Execution protocol**

> - Read this Global Mandatory Reading section before the first task and again

> - A referenced heading or exported symbol is authoritative. Global navigation

>   the named section or symbol rather than expanding to the whole file.
> - Mandatory reading is non-transitive. Do not preload a linked file's own
>   Mandatory Reading, Consult on Demand, or Planning Provenance entries unless
>   this plan names them directly for the current area.
> - Do not read Consult on Demand or Planning Provenance merely because those

>   basenames and link them here with `../../../../logs/<basename>.log`.
>   Earlier documentation logs were removed by the user; retain historical
>   result summaries without recreating those files. Markdown notes and scratch
>   scripts still belong beside their owning plan.
> - No created source file, test file or JSON file should start with same prefix as a planning MD file (such as r2). Only MD files can have that prefix. No MD file should be created anywhere in source directories unless specifically asked.
> - After EVERY CONTEXT COMPACTION, you must add a note above the task group
>   which is currently in execution. The note will say: "Context has compacted
>   X times while executing this group.". If the compaction occurs before any
>   meaningful work was done (this can happen when there is a lot of baggage
>   from the previous compaction), then and ONLY then you can skip this step.
> - When exceeding 3 context compactions, YOU MUST ask yourself if you're not
>   chewing more than we can handle :). Heck, plans change! :). In such a case,
>   the way out is to split the group on the spot,
>   in the plan, into more groups,
>   by appending B, C, D as a suffixes to those groups (splitting sub-groups
>   themselves is also allowed). When you do this, create Local Mandatory
>   Reading areas so you can optimize your context only for the
>   ACTUAL task at hand.
>   Before starting or resuming a task group, read the nearest preceding Area
>   Mandatory Reading block whose `Applies to` label includes that group. Area
>   blocks appear immediately before the first group they govern.

**Prompt camera clarification,2026-10-20.** Native `RemapCoords` requires projected world-axis slopes26.565degrees, horizontal:vertical2:1. For an orthographic3D camera at45degree azimuth, this means camera elevation30degrees (`sin(30)=0.5`), not26.565degrees elevation and not true-isometric35.264degrees elevation. Earlier requests ambiguously called the projected axis angle the camera angle and sometimes banned30degrees; retain their exact historical provenance but use the explicit correct elevation/azimuth for new source renders. Accepted originals remain protected and still need their actual native pixel/contact checks.

## Completion Conditions

Every required building, vehicle, resource and gameplay-critical state has packaged graphics, valid directions/anchors and a runtime capture. Repeatable conversion works from retained sources. Offline sheets, generated originals and a successful compiler run alone do not establish art acceptance.
